# Statistical relationship inventory — support A, passes 1 and 2

Runtime ID: 01a12886-83ee-7ac0-afea-c841f69920e7; model gpt-6.1-sol; effort high; FRESH_SPAWN. Exact disjoint scope S001–S120: supplement eTable4p6, eTable5p7, eFigure1p8, eTable6p9, eTable7pp10/19. Exactly120relationships,104unique scalar estimate/95%CI tuples,16additional interaction/PH tests. Each record is PASS_1_COMPLETE. Pass 2 completed by distinct fresh runtime 01a12893-5344-7fb2-a4f4-e1a8e8ac9e7c; gpt-6.1-sol/high; FRESH_SPAWN. All original pass-1 values/proof are preserved. Candidate aliases STA-A-01 and STA-A-02 are discovery records only, pending coordinator stable registration and human adjudication.

## Source definitions and bounded inferential limits

- **A — rate difference:** REPORT supplement p6 eTable4 title, rate columns and footnote b specify nonsurgical minus surgical event rates per100patient-years at8years and95% bootstrap CIs from1000samples. Compare displayed subtraction, interval ordering/containment, positive direction and rate units. No P, bootstrap sample values or exact CI construction algorithm are printed on that page. Main p4 specifies percentile bootstrap for absolute *risk* differences; do not silently transfer that rule to rate differences. The parenthesized rates for five individual endpoints are death/outcome composite rates, not confidence endpoints.
- **B — cumulative incidence:** REPORT supplement p7 eTable5 specifies percent,95%CI, years2/5/8 and both treatments; REPORT main p4 defines Kaplan–Meier cumulative incidence. Check containment,0–100 bounds, monotonic point estimates and surgical/nonsurgical direction. The variance estimator/transform and unrounded values for each CI are not supplied; no P is printed in eTable5, so do not test an individual marginal interval against the overall Cox association P. All16year8 tuples repeat without value differences in REPORT main p7 Table2; trailing-zero precision is compatible.
- **C — subgroup HR:** REPORT supplement p8 eFigure1 specifies adjusted HR,95%CI, primary composite, surgery versus usual care, null1 and decreased/increased-risk arrows. Its footnote specifies replacing the original covariate by the dichotomous version and treatment interaction; age/eGFR use medians. All16graphic markers/bars agree with printed left-of-null direction. Rounded identical values for distinct subgroups are not an identity contradiction. No within-subgroup association P, SE, coefficient covariance or unrounded coefficients are printed.
- **D — interaction P:** REPORT supplement p8 explicitly identifies the eight P values as subgroup-variable × surgical-indicator interactions in the modified fully adjusted Cox model. REPORT main p4 states two-sided comparisons and no interaction heterogeneity. All eight P values exceed0.05. They are not the association P values for the sixteen within-subgroup HRs. Exact test statistic, df, contrast covariance, variance estimator and pooling rule for each interaction are not printed; do not infer an independent-subgroup z-test from the two marginal CIs.
- **E — overall Cox association:** REPORT supplement p9 eTable6 and REPORT main p7 Table2 print identical HR/95%CI/association-P triples. REPORT main p4 supplies fully adjusted Cox models, baseline covariates, five imputed datasets, Rubin SE, alpha0.05 and two-sided comparisons; PLAN protocol p6 corroborates model/SE definitions. Printed intervals all exclude1 downward and association P values are below0.05. Exact CI/test statistic construction, pooled df and covariance or variance details are not supplied. This permits direct qualitative consistency and repeated-value checks; it does not establish a unique numerical interval-to-P inversion. No asymptotic normal P reconstruction is used as evidence.
- **F — PH P:** REPORT supplement p9 footnote identifies a proportional-hazards-assumption test for each outcome; REPORT main p4 and PLAN protocol p6 identify Grambsch–Therneau weighted residuals for the treatment term. PH P is not the Cox treatment-association P or a test of HR=1. No weighted-residual statistic, df, transformation or exact pooling implementation is supplied. AF PH P0.04 is a reported diagnostic result; a departure from PH does not contradict the existence of an overall estimate or time-varying sensitivity estimate.
- **G — time-varying HR:** REPORT supplement pp10/19 eTable7 prints95%CI for adjusted surgery/nonsurgical HRs at years2/5/8. Footnote/prose specifies treatment interacted with a restricted cubic spline on observed follow-up time. REPORT main p4 similarly describes a time-spline treatment interaction; PLAN protocol p6 specifies these time points. All24tuples repeat exactly between pp10/19. The constant-HR model is a different model; do not require its numerical estimate to equal an individual time-varying estimate. No time-specific P, SE, spline coefficients/covariance, df, knots or full CI construction rule are supplied. AF8-year upper endpoint1.00 is a rounded endpoint, not proof of an exact-null bound.

Source roles are assigned by content: the article and results supplement are REPORT; prospective protocol is PLAN. Definitions supplied by PLAN only corroborate compatible REPORT methods and do not create a conflicting role relationship. Source authority is the unchanged PDFs; raw text and reused images are locators/transcription aids. Direct Poppler26.01.0 `pdftotext -layout -f 6 -l 10 joi190103supp1_prod.pdf -` and `-f 19 -l 19` confirmed scalar tables, units and both label observations. Reused p8 image was visually inspected for all16labels, values and bars; native text has no embedded scalar figure values. Reused p6/p19 images confirm page layout. No newly persisted derivative, OCR, Python extraction or web use.

Rules use printed precision; no unrounded estimate, estimand mapping, sidedness, covariance, variance estimator, multiplicity, denominator or df is inferred from convention. No assigned P value is displayed as zero; DISPLAY_ZERO_NOT_CANDIDATE is therefore NOT_APPLICABLE. No diagnostic tail-probability calculation is made.

## Source-grounded definition and limitation keys

**D-CONT:** eTable 8 footnote specifies four-knot spline interacted with treatment for comparisons of mean changes in metabolic/nutritional variables. Table title identifies surgery vs nonsurgery and baseline-change context. S121–S148 use signed between-group difference on the printed variable's scale, null 0. Main Methods [DOC-001 REPORT p.4](../../../jama_aminian_2019_oi_190103.pdf#page=4) specifies two-sided comparisons; spline model covariance, time-point contrast matrix, within-person variance/correlation, inferential distribution/degrees of freedom, exact confidence-interval formula and unrounded results are not supplied. The Bonferroni confidence level is printed as 98.8%; exact unrounded level, adjustment family and meaning of stars are not explicitly defined. Do not substitute normal-Wald inversion for the reported model.

**D-MED:** eTable 8 footnote specifies two-sample proportions test at each time point; main Methods [DOC-001 REPORT p.4](../../../jama_aminian_2019_oi_190103.pdf#page=4) specifies years 1,2,5,8 and two-sided comparisons. S149–S172 use a difference in proportions on a percentage-point scale, surgery vs nonsurgery, null 0; these are not HRs, relative changes or person-time rates. eTable 10 p14 supplies group sample sizes 1820/10309,1444/8762,784/4235,348/1219 at these times, but drug-specific numerator counts and exact proportions are not printed. Exact implementation, continuity correction, pooled versus unpooled variance, difference-CI construction, same-test/CI inversion relation, P-value multiplicity treatment, unrounded confidence level, adjustment family and star rule are not supplied. A generic two-sample test label is not sufficient authority to assign a z test. TEST_LABEL_SPECIFICITY_LIMIT records the differing specificity of source labels at the six 8-year repetitions: a generic two-sample proportions test can include Fisher exact, and no supplied assertion identifies a contradictory algorithm. This is no-candidate coverage, not a numerical P disagreement.

**D-KM:** eTable 11 title/footnote specify postoperative cumulative incidence (%) by Kaplan–Meier, 95% CI, surgical cohort only, years1,2,5,8. No inferential comparison or P/statistic/SE is printed. Event counts, endpoint risk sets, exact follow-up denominators, censoring, variance estimator, transformation and interval-production rule are not printed for these interventions. Do not reconstruct binomial counts from KM percentages; intervention categories can overlap. Abdominal surgical procedure explicitly excludes hernia repair and cholecystectomy.

**D-HR:** eTable 12 labels known-factor HRs with 95% CIs in the fully-adjusted Cox-model context, for magnitude comparison. Each printed reference contrast below is authoritative; do not standardize its direction merely because other endpoints use different references. HR scale is positive, null1. No P value, statistic or SE for these known factors is printed. Their individual model coefficient/covariance records, exact variance/CI estimator and imputation-combination degrees of freedom are not supplied. Main p4 gives general five-dataset multiple imputation and Rubin SE definitions, but that does not supply coefficient-specific SE or a test/CI inversion formula. These tuples are distinct from surgery-vs-nonsurgery treatment HRs and RR-scale E-values.

## Shared source definitions and limits D1

The figure title supplies fully-adjusted Cox models, hazard ratios and 95% confidence intervals for metabolic surgery versus no surgery. The prose supplies five index-date assignments, three ratios, 15 datasets and significance counts. HR=1 is the plotted no-association comparator; every point is below it and every plotted lower/upper position contains its point. The axis is labelled Hazard ratio (95% CI), with evenly spaced printed ticks 0.0 through 1.2; graphical zero is an axis tick, not a reported zero estimate or P value.

Exact HR and endpoint decimals, dataset-specific P values, test statistics, standard errors, sidedness, degrees of freedom, confidence-limit construction, variance estimator, multiplicity treatment, and the exact CI/P-value testing relation are not supplied on the assigned pages. Fully-adjusted Cox is a model-family label, not a complete definition of inferential compatibility. No convention is substituted for these missing inputs. Comparisons between observed null crossings and significance counts below are explicitly diagnostic graphical consistency checks, not reconstructed P values or definitive test equivalence. All nine intervals visibly cross the null beyond cap/line thickness; the other 111 upper limits lie visibly below it. The upper cap for Afib ratio1:1 iteration1 is at or beyond the upper panel edge; its plotted interval visibly extends above HR=1, but its exact upper limit is not recoverable.

Exact equality, duplication, or rounding reconciliation across nearby graphical points cannot be determined without printed scalar inputs. No identical-value candidate is emitted from overlap alone. Ratio labels repeat within the figure consistently, but the prose/legend order differs; checker alias GRAPH-P1-01 records that shared labeling comparison.

## Common inferential definitions and exact limits

These named profiles are part of every record that invokes them; per-record values, population, time, and outcome override only where explicitly stated.

- **KM profile:** DOC-001 Methods p4: Kaplan–Meier cumulative incidence at eight years after index; percent, not event-count fraction or person-time rate. Group-specific estimate and 95% CI. Model is Kaplan–Meier, not adjusted Cox. No test or P value is separately printed for the individual group estimate. General comparisons are two-sided at alpha .05, but sidedness is not a property of a standalone estimate. CI transform, pointwise variance formula and numeric SE are not stated. No multiplicity adjustment is specified for these CIs. Outcome-specific at-risk population excludes prior same nonfatal condition; composite and mortality retain the full cohort. Main p3 supplies this risk-set rule.
- **ARD profile:** DOC-001 Methods p4 and Table2 footnote a p7: eight-year nonsurgical-minus-surgical absolute risk difference; percentage points. 95% percentile bootstrap CI from 1000 iterations/samples. No separate ARD P/statistic/SE is supplied. Two-sided comparisons alpha .05 is general; bootstrap resampling unit, joint covariance, seeds and full-precision KM estimates are not supplied. No stated multiplicity adjustment for these CIs. Compare displayed ARD to matched KM estimates using their printed rounding ranges; do not equate this CI with the adjusted-Cox association test.
- **Cox profile:** DOC-001 Methods p4 and Table2 footnote b p7: surgery versus nonsurgical instantaneous-risk HR from fully adjusted Cox PH model, all baseline Table1 variables included. Endpoint-specific eligible populations as in KM profile; follow-up from index until endpoint/censoring, not a fixed-time RR. 95% CI; two-sided comparisons alpha .05; five MICE datasets for baseline missingness; Rubin formula for imputation-corrected SEs. Secondary analyses exploratory; no secondary-endpoint multiplicity adjustment claimed. Numeric SE, coefficient, association-test family (Wald/LR/score), pooled reference distribution/df, covariance and variance estimator for matching are not supplied. Hence exact inversion of CI to P is unavailable. CI null exclusion and P<.05 agree in every main endpoint. A normal/log-Wald calculation would be a diagnostic only, not the reported test.
- **Spline profile:** DOC-001 p4 Methods and p9 Figure4 caption: flexible regression, four-knot spline of time since index interacted with treatment, comparing changes from baseline at eight years; model population is longitudinally observed surgical/nonsurgical patients (sample-size counterparts DOC-002 eTable9 p13). 95% main CI; general two-sided alpha .05; no numeric SE/statistic, exact regression family/link/covariance, knot positions, test distribution/df or longitudinal missingness model printed. Baseline MICE/Rubin rule is stated globally, but its application to these longitudinal estimates is not specified. DOC-002 eTable8 p12 explicitly prints 98.8% Bonferroni-corrected CIs, so main 95% and support 98.8% intervals are not interchangeable. Contrast sign may be a reduction magnitude in main versus signed surgery-minus-control change in supplement.
- **Medication profile:** DOC-001 p4 Methods: two-sample proportions test at 1,2,5,8 years; DOC-001 Figure5 caption p10: Fisher exact test at eight years. DOC-002 eTable8 p12: two-sample proportions test and 98.8% Bonferroni CIs; eTable10 p14 at eight years n=348 surgery,1219 nonsurgical, but per-drug numerators not supplied. General two-sided alpha .05; displayed Figure5 group bands are 95% pointwise CIs at every tenth of a year through eight years. No exact band formula, test options/continuity correction, per-drug numerators, numeric SE or statistic. The generic two-sample-proportions label does not establish an incompatible implementation with Fisher exact; record TEST_LABEL_SPECIFICITY_LIMIT, not a candidate. No P recalculation or unsupported denominator substitution is made.
- **Trend profile:** DOC-002 eFigure2 p11 and eFigure3 p16 show treatment curves and shaded bands; source pages do not define shading confidence level, band formula, exact scalar estimates/endpoints, numeric SE, tests/P, model adjustment or sample counts for each curve. General four-knot spline mean-change analysis is elsewhere, but curve-specific fitting/estimand mapping is not explicitly supplied. Review graph identities, axes, trend direction, and visible curve containment only; do not digitize into exact data or equate raw mean levels with adjusted differences in changes.
- **E-value profile:** DOC-002 p19 definition/eTable12 p20 and DOC-003 PLAN p7: minimum confounder association with treatment and outcome conditional on measured covariates, on RR scale; calculated for adjusted treatment HR and closest-to-null 95% CI limit, eight endpoints. Because all treatment HRs and upper limits are below one, upper limit is closest to null. E-values are sensitivity thresholds, not estimates with a CI/P; they inherit outcome-specific adjusted-Cox population/follow-up. Exact HR-to-RR conversion, common/rare-outcome rule, required baseline risk/time, calculation algorithm/software and full-precision inputs are not supplied. All paired E-values are >1 and point E exceeds limit E. Primary E2.15/1.92 repeats consistently p19/p20. No formula-based discrepancy is manufactured.

## Main Table 2: 32 distinct tuples

## S357 — Primary extended six-component MACE — surgical KM

- **Source and role:** [DOC-001 Table2 p7](../../../jama_aminian_2019_oi_190103.pdf#page=7) (REPORT); matching [DOC-002 eTable5 p7](../../../joi190103supp1_prod.pdf#page=7) (REPORT).
- **Population/time/contrast:** surgery n=2287, nonsurgical n=11435; full matched cohort for this endpoint; eight years after index; surgical cumulative incidence.
- **Printed inference:** Estimate 30.8%; 95% CI 27.6–34.0%; P/test not separately supplied. **Model/scale/sidedness/test/variance/adjustment and missing definitions:** KM profile above; no numeric SE/statistic supplied.
- **Pass 1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE. CI endpoints ordered; point contained; percent scale and lower surgery-risk direction coherent. Matched supplement year8 values agree. No candidate. **Pass 2:** PASS_2_COMPLETE. Primary six-component surgical KM point and CI remain contained/ordered; matched year-8 supplement repeats agree. Endpoint-specific risk set and censoring remain distinct from enrolled or medication analytic denominators and person-time rates. No new candidate; KM transform/variance and full-precision estimates remain unspecified.

## S359 — Primary extended six-component MACE — ARD

- **Source and role:** [DOC-001 Table2 p7](../../../jama_aminian_2019_oi_190103.pdf#page=7) (REPORT); matching [DOC-002 eTable5 p7](../../../joi190103supp1_prod.pdf#page=7) (REPORT).
- **Population/time/contrast:** surgery n=2287, nonsurgical n=11435; full matched cohort for this endpoint; eight years after index; nonsurgical minus surgery absolute risk.
- **Printed inference:** Estimate 16.9 percentage points; 95% CI 13.1 to 20.4; P/test not separately supplied. **Model/scale/sidedness/test/variance/adjustment and missing definitions:** ARD profile above; no numeric SE/statistic supplied.
- **Pass 1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE. CI endpoints ordered and contain point; displayed subtraction 47.7−30.8=16.9 agrees. No candidate. **Pass 2:** PASS_2_COMPLETE. Same-row REPORT subtraction 47.7 minus 30.8 = 16.9 percentage points reconciles, with point contained in ordered bootstrap CI. Outcome/group/time/population labels remain matched; bootstrap ARD is distinct from Cox inference and rate differences. ## Main unquantified inferential claims: five complete linked relationships

## S400 — Primary subgroup interaction/no heterogeneity

- **Source and role:** DOC-001 pp4 Methods and Results; DOC-002 eFigure1 p8; DOC-001 and DOC-002 REPORT; DOC-003 PLAN when explicitly named. All PDF locations are physical pages; [main p4](../../../jama_aminian_2019_oi_190103.pdf#page=4), [main p6](../../../jama_aminian_2019_oi_190103.pdf#page=6), [support p19](../../../joi190103supp1_prod.pdf#page=19), [PLANp6](../../../joi190103supp2_prod.pdf#page=6), [PLANp7](../../../joi190103supp2_prod.pdf#page=7) resolve the linked definitions.
- **Population/time/contrast:** primary composite matched cohort, surgery versus usual care; entire eligible follow-up; eight individually added treatment×variable interactions.
- **Printed inference:** Interaction P=.35,.93,.90,.80,.94,.53,.72,.98 for sex,age,BMI,HbA1c,eGFR,insulin,sulfonylurea,lipid-lowering use respectively.95% subgroup HR CIs shown in support. **Model/scale/sidedness/test/variance/adjustment and missing definitions:** Fully adjusted Cox interaction models; subgroup covariate substituted by dichotomy plus interaction in eFigure1; general two-sided alpha.05; baseline MICE/Rubin SE definition. Interaction-test family, covariance between subgroup effects, statistic, df and exact SE absent. Interaction P is not an individual subgroup-HR association P; do not reconstruct it from overlapping subgroup CIs.
- **Pass 1:** PASS_1_COMPLETE. All interaction P>.05 match no detected heterogeneity; no candidate. Support scalar tuples are cross-references, not reassigned S IDs. **Pass 2:** PASS_2_COMPLETE. All eight supplied interaction Ps exceed .05 and agree with no detected heterogeneity; no candidate follows from differing subgroup HR intervals. Treatment×subgroup interaction and within-subgroup association are different hypotheses. Counts/populations do not establish an interaction covariance. No new candidate; exact interaction test/df/covariance/SE absent.

## S437 — PLANp6 statistical definitions
